多患者20分钟EEG：候选与局部证据

3名患者，6段×20分钟。公开标注用于选取展示片段及事后核对，起止答案不进入Agent推理。不是盲测、完整benchmark或临床诊断。

蓝线为线长变化比；绿带为参考区间；橙带为Agent疑似；紫带为不确定（未作为阳性计分）。宽松筛选候选不直接等于发作；每条候选必须完成局部时域、频谱、跨通道证据复核。

案例01 / chb02

状态：completed；参考区间：[[572.0, 653.0]]；疑似区间：[]；TP/FP/FN：0/0/1

C1 | uncertain | 0.0–180.0秒

时域 / C1.temporal：The temporal evidence shows a decrease in RMS and line-length ratios across all channels during the interval, indicating a potential change in activity. However, the change is not sustained or rhythmic, and the ratios return to baseline levels after the interval.

频谱 / C1.spectral：During the interval, there is an increase in delta power and a decrease in alpha and beta power across multiple channels. However, these changes are not consistent across all channels and do not show a clear pattern of rhythmic evolution. The spectral entropy and peak fraction also show some variation, but not enough to confirm a seizure.

跨通道 / C1.cross_channel：The cross-channel correlations during the interval are not significantly different from the baseline, and some correlations even show a decrease. This suggests that the changes are not widespread or synchronized across channels, which is not typical of a seizure.

替代解释：The changes observed could be due to a physiological state change or an artifact. The lack of sustained rhythmic evolution and the absence of clear spatial support suggest that the changes are not indicative of a seizure.

C2 | uncertain | 160.0–305.0秒

时域 / C2.temporal：The temporal evidence shows a sustained increase in RMS and line-length ratios across the channels CZ-PZ, P8-O2, C4-P4, and P4-O2 during the interval [160.0, 305.0]. The RMS ratio increased from 1.4 to 2.1899, and the line-length ratio increased from 1.7 to 1.7634. However, the support is not consistent throughout the entire interval, with some periods showing a decrease in ratios. The temporal evidence does not show a clear and sustained evolution that would support a seizure.

频谱 / C2.spectral：The spectral evidence shows a shift in the dominant frequency from 16.0 Hz to 2.5 Hz during the interval [160.0, 305.0]. The delta relative power increased from 0.0493 to 0.3604, and the theta relative power increased from 0.0582 to 0.1736. However, the spectral changes are not consistent throughout the interval, with some periods showing a decrease in delta and theta relative power. The spectral evidence does not show a clear and sustained evolution that would support a seizure.

跨通道 / C2.cross_channel：The cross-channel evidence shows an increase in pairwise correlations between the channels CZ-PZ, P8-O2, C4-P4, and P4-O2 during the interval [160.0, 305.0]. The pairwise correlations increased from -0.0442 to 0.0497, and the shared electrodes can create correlation. However, the cross-channel evidence does not show a clear and sustained evolution that would support a seizure.

替代解释：The changes observed in the temporal, spectral, and cross-channel evidence could be due to physiological state changes or artifacts. The evidence does not show a clear and sustained evolution that would support a seizure, and the changes are not consistent throughout the entire interval.

C3 | uncertain | 315.0–495.0秒

时域 / C3.temporal：The temporal evidence shows a gradual increase in RMS and line length ratios across the channels during the interval, with a peak around 315-320 seconds. The ratios then decrease slightly but remain elevated compared to the baseline. This suggests a possible change in activity, but the pattern is not clearly sustained or rhythmic.

频谱 / C3.spectral：The spectral analysis indicates a significant increase in delta power during the interval, particularly in the FP1-F3 and FP2-F4 channels. The dominant frequency remains around 2.0 Hz, but the spectral entropy decreases, suggesting a more concentrated power distribution. However, the changes are not consistently sustained across all channels, and the peak fraction remains relatively high, which could indicate a physiological state change rather than a seizure.

跨通道 / C3.cross_channel：The cross-channel correlations show a decrease in correlation between the channels during the interval, particularly between P3-O1 and FP1-F3. This suggests a possible change in the synchronization of the activity, but the correlation values are not consistently low across all pairs. The shared electrodes may have influenced the correlation values, so this should be interpreted with caution.

替代解释：The changes observed could be due to a physiological state change or an artifact. The spectral changes are not consistently sustained across all channels, and the correlation values are not consistently low. Therefore, the evidence is not sufficient to confirm a seizure.

C4 | uncertain | 475.0–655.0秒

时域 / C4.temporal：The temporal evidence shows a sustained increase in RMS and line length ratios across multiple channels during the interval [475.0, 655.0]. However, the changes are not consistently sustained across the entire interval, with some periods showing a return to baseline levels. The temporal evidence does not show a clear, sustained evolution that would support a seizure.

频谱 / C4.spectral：The spectral evidence indicates a shift in the dominant frequency and changes in spectral entropy and peak fraction during the interval. However, these changes are not consistently sustained across the entire interval, and the spectral changes do not show a clear, rhythmic evolution that would support a seizure. The spectral evidence is not sufficient to confirm a seizure.

跨通道 / C4.cross_channel：The cross-channel evidence shows some correlation changes during the interval, but these changes are not consistent across all channels. The correlation changes are not sufficient to confirm a seizure. Shared electrodes can create correlation, and focal seizures need not be globally synchronous.

替代解释：The changes observed could be due to physiological state changes or artifacts. The evidence does not show a clear, sustained evolution that would support a seizure. The changes are not specific to seizure activity and could be due to other factors.

C1 | uncertain | 0.0–60.0秒

时域 / C1.temporal：The temporal evidence shows a broad RMS and line-length increase across multiple channels during the interval [0.0, 60.0]. The RMS ratios for C3-P3, F3-C3, T7-P7, and P3-O1 are above 1.0, with the highest at 1.4856 for C3-P3. The line-length ratios are also elevated, with the highest at 1.5097 for F3-C3. These changes are sustained and consistent across the interval, indicating a potential change in activity.

频谱 / C1.spectral：The spectral evidence during the interval [0.0, 60.0] shows elevated RMS and line-length values compared to the before and after periods. For example, the RMS_uv for F3-C3 is 18.2342 during the interval, which is higher than the after period value of 16.8033. The delta_rel for F3-C3 is 0.5234 during the interval, which is higher than the after period value of 0.5956. These changes suggest a possible increase in slow wave activity, but the spectral entropy and peak fraction are within normal ranges, indicating that this may not be a seizure.

跨通道 / C1.cross_channel：The cross-channel evidence shows some correlation between channels during the interval [0.0, 60.0]. For example, the correlation between C3-P3 and F3-C3 is 0.261 during the interval, which is lower than the after period value of 0.4422. However, the correlation between C3-P3 and T7-P7 is -0.0478 during the interval, which is lower than the after period value of 0.3238. These changes suggest some level of synchronization, but the correlation is not strong enough to indicate a seizure.

替代解释：The changes observed could be due to a physiological state change or an artifact. The spectral entropy and peak fraction are within normal ranges, and the changes are not sustained across all channels. Therefore, the evidence is not sufficient to confirm a seizure.

C2 | uncertain | 295.0–325.0秒

时域 / C2.temporal：The temporal evidence shows a sustained increase in RMS and line length ratios across the selected channels during the interval [295.0, 325.0]. The RMS ratios for FP1-F3, FP2-F8, FP2-F4, and FZ-CZ increased from 0.5393 to 0.9174, 0.7334 to 1.1377, 0.4153 to 0.7466, and 0.3525 to 0.5656, respectively. This indicates a consistent elevation in activity levels during the candidate interval.

频谱 / C2.spectral：The spectral evidence reveals a notable change in the delta relative power during the candidate interval. For FP1-F3, the delta_rel increased from 0.7505 to 0.774, for FP2-F8 from 0.716 to 0.783, for FP2-F4 from 0.7271 to 0.7321, and for FZ-CZ from 0.7228 to 0.7746. This suggests a possible shift in the spectral power distribution, though the changes are not sufficiently pronounced to confirm a seizure.

跨通道 / C2.cross_channel：The cross-channel evidence shows a decrease in pairwise correlations during the candidate interval. For example, the correlation between FP1-F3 and FP2-F8 decreased from 0.8974 to 0.8746, and between FP1-F3 and FP2-F4 decreased from 0.9655 to 0.9406. This indicates a potential loss of synchronization, which could be a sign of a seizure, but the changes are not consistent across all channels.

替代解释：The observed changes could be due to a physiological state change or an artifact. The spectral changes are not sufficiently pronounced to confirm a seizure, and the cross-channel correlations are not consistently altered across all channels. Therefore, the evidence is inconclusive, and the candidate remains uncertain.

C3 | uncertain | 325.0–505.0秒

时域 / C3.temporal：The temporal evidence shows a sustained increase in RMS and line length ratios across multiple channels (T7-P7, P8-O2, T8-P8-0, P4-O2) during the interval [325.0, 505.0]. The ratios are consistently above 1.2, indicating a notable change in activity. However, the change is not abrupt and does not show a clear transition from baseline to seizure activity.

频谱 / C3.spectral：The spectral analysis reveals a shift in dominant frequency and increased delta power during the interval. The dominant frequency increases from around 2.0 Hz to 2.5 Hz, and the delta relative power increases. However, the changes are not sufficiently sustained or rhythmic to confirm a seizure. The spectral entropy also shows some variation, but it does not indicate a clear seizure-specific pattern.

跨通道 / C3.cross_channel：The cross-channel correlations show some changes, with a decrease in correlation between P8-O2 and T8-P8-0 during the interval. However, the correlation values are not significantly different from baseline, and the changes are not consistent across all channels. Shared electrodes may have influenced the correlation values, and the changes do not indicate a clear focal seizure.

替代解释：The changes observed could be due to a physiological state change or an artifact. The sustained increase in RMS and line length ratios, along with changes in spectral properties, could indicate a non-seizure-related event. Further analysis is needed to determine the exact cause of the changes.

C4 | uncertain | 485.0–575.0秒

时域 / C4.temporal：The temporal evidence shows a sustained increase in RMS and line-length ratios across the selected channels (P4-O2, P8-O2, P3-O1, P7-O1) during the interval [485.0, 575.0]. The ratios are consistently above the baseline, indicating a potential change in activity. However, the change is not abrupt and does not show a clear transition from baseline to the candidate interval.

频谱 / C4.spectral：The spectral analysis reveals a shift in the dominant frequency and spectral entropy during the candidate interval. The dominant frequency decreases from around 2.5 Hz to 2.0 Hz, and the spectral entropy increases slightly. These changes are not isolated but are sustained throughout the interval, suggesting a possible change in the underlying neural activity. However, the changes are not sufficiently specific to indicate a seizure.

跨通道 / C4.cross_channel：The cross-channel correlations show a decrease in correlation values during the candidate interval, which may indicate a change in the synchronization of neural activity. However, the decrease is not consistent across all channels and may be influenced by shared electrodes. The correlation values do not provide strong evidence for a seizure.

替代解释：The changes observed could be due to a physiological state change or an artifact. The sustained increase in RMS and line-length ratios, along with the shift in dominant frequency and spectral entropy, could indicate a change in neural activity. However, without further evidence, it is not possible to confirm that this is a seizure.

C3 | uncertain | 560.0–600.0秒

时域 / C3.temporal：The temporal evidence shows a decrease in RMS and line length ratios during the interval, with a notable drop in RMS ratio from 1.5168 to 1.1329 and line length ratio from 1.7635 to 1.0949. This indicates a reduction in activity, which may suggest a change in the underlying neural dynamics.

频谱 / C3.spectral：The spectral analysis reveals a significant increase in delta relative power during the interval, with values rising from 0.5831 to 0.7153 for P7-O1 and from 0.6159 to 0.6336 for P8-O2. This suggests a shift in power distribution, potentially indicating a change in brain activity. However, the increase in delta power alone is not sufficient to confirm a seizure.

跨通道 / C3.cross_channel：The cross-channel correlations show a decrease in correlation between P7-O1 and T7-P7 during the interval, from 0.1273 to -0.3848, indicating a potential change in the synchronization of neural activity. However, the presence of shared electrodes may inflate these correlations, and the decrease in correlation alone is not sufficient to confirm a seizure.

替代解释：The observed changes could be due to a physiological state change or an artifact. The increase in delta power and the decrease in RMS and line length ratios could be indicative of a change in brain activity, but without further evidence of sustained rhythmic evolution or clear transition, it is uncertain whether this represents a seizure.

时域 / C4.temporal：The temporal evidence shows a sustained increase in RMS and line-length ratios across the channels during the interval [690.0, 745.0]. The RMS ratios for FT10-T8, T8-P8-0, T7-P7, and T7-FT9 increased from 1.0795 to 1.2258, 1.1654, and 1.1679, respectively, indicating a notable change in amplitude. The line-length ratios also increased, suggesting a potential change in the signal's complexity or rhythmicity.

频谱 / C4.spectral：The spectral analysis reveals a shift in the dominant frequency and spectral entropy during the interval. The dominant frequency decreased from 2.75 to 2.0 Hz, and the spectral entropy increased from 0.6615 to 0.7325, indicating a potential change in the signal's complexity. The peak fraction also increased, suggesting a more concentrated spectral power during the interval.

跨通道 / C4.cross_channel：The cross-channel analysis shows a decrease in pairwise correlations during the interval, which may indicate a change in the signal's synchrony. However, the shared electrodes may have influenced the correlation values, so this evidence should be interpreted with caution.

替代解释：The changes observed could be due to a physiological state change or an artifact. The spectral changes could also be due to a shift in the dominant frequency or an increase in the signal's complexity. Further analysis is needed to determine the exact cause of the changes.

案例04 / chb02

状态：completed；参考区间：[]；疑似区间：[]；TP/FP/FN：0/0/0

C1 | uncertain | 290.0–310.0秒

时域 / C1.temporal：The RMS ratio and line length ratio for the channels P7-O1, F3-C3, P8-O2, and P4-O2 show a notable increase during the interval 290-310 seconds compared to the before and after intervals. This suggests a potential local change in activity, but the changes are not sustained or rhythmic enough to confirm a seizure.

频谱 / C1.spectral：The spectral analysis shows an increase in beta and gamma relative power during the interval, with a decrease in delta and theta relative power. However, these changes are not consistent across all channels and do not show a clear pattern of sustained rhythmic evolution. The spectral entropy and peak fraction also show some variation, but not enough to confirm a seizure.

跨通道 / C1.cross_channel：The pairwise correlations between the channels show an increase in correlation during the interval, but this is not consistent across all pairs. The shared electrodes may have inflated the correlation, and the changes are not sufficient to confirm a seizure.

替代解释：The changes observed could be due to a physiological state change or an artifact. The spectral changes are not specific to seizures and could be due to other factors. The correlation changes could be due to shared electrodes or other non-seizure-related factors.

C1 | suspected | 350.0–530.0秒

时域 / C1.temporal：The temporal evidence shows a significant increase in RMS and line length ratios across multiple channels during the candidate interval (350-530 seconds). For example, the RMS ratio for T8-P8-0 increased from 2.8064 to 3.2047, and the line length ratio increased from 3.2047 to 3.1029. These changes are sustained and show a clear evolution over the interval.

频谱 / C1.spectral：The spectral evidence indicates a notable change in the power distribution during the candidate interval. The delta relative power increased from 0.2584 to 0.3784 for T8-P8-0, and the alpha relative power decreased from 0.3934 to 0.0494. These changes are consistent across multiple channels and show a clear shift in the spectral profile.

跨通道 / C1.cross_channel：The cross-channel evidence shows a significant increase in correlation between channels during the candidate interval. For example, the correlation between FP1-F3 and FP2-F4 increased from 0.5509 to 0.807, indicating a more synchronized activity pattern. However, shared electrodes can inflate correlation, so this should be interpreted with caution.

替代解释：The changes observed could be due to a physiological state change or an artifact. However, the sustained rhythmic evolution and clear transition in the temporal and spectral evidence suggest a more likely seizure activity.

This indicates a significant change in the signal amplitude and line length, which could suggest a potential seizure. However, the change is not consistent across all channels, and the increase is not sustained throughout the entire interval.

频谱 / C2.spectral：The spectral evidence shows a decrease in delta power and an increase in theta and beta power during the interval [510.0, 545.0]. 跨通道 / C2.cross_channel：The cross-channel evidence shows a decrease in correlation between the channels during the interval [510.0, 545.0]. The correlation between FT9-FT10 and P7-O1 decreased from 0.4446 to 0.297, while the correlation between FT9-FT10 and P8-O2 decreased from -0.2616 to -0.3761. This suggests a potential change in the synchronization of the signal across the channels, which could be indicative of a seizure. However, the change is not consistent across all channels, and the decrease in correlation is not sustained throughout the entire interval.

替代解释：The changes observed in the temporal, spectral, and cross-channel evidence could be due to a variety of factors, including physiological changes, artifacts, or other non-seizure-related events. The changes are not consistent across all channels, and the increase in RMS and line length ratios is not sustained throughout the entire interval. Therefore, the evidence is not sufficient to confirm a seizure.

C3 | uncertain | 740.0–770.0秒

时域 / C3.temporal：The temporal evidence shows a sustained increase in RMS and line-length ratios across the selected channels (P8-O2, P7-O1, P4-O2, C4-P4) during the interval [740.0, 770.0]. The ratios increased from 1.1586 to 1.4245 for P8-O2, 1.2853 to 1.3467 for P7-O1, 1.1586 to 1.3467 for P4-O2, and 1.3467 to 1.3467 for C4-P4. This indicates a consistent change in the signal amplitude and line length, which could suggest a potential seizure activity.

频谱 / C3.spectral：The spectral evidence shows a decrease in delta relative power (from 0.4112 to 0.4032 for P8-O2, 0.5348 to 0.5499 for P7-O1, 0.4109 to 0.4546 for P4-O2, and 0.584 to 0.7034 for C4-P4) during the interval [740.0, 770.0]. This decrease in delta power is accompanied by an increase in alpha and beta relative power, which could indicate a shift in brain activity. However, the changes are not consistent across all channels, and the spectral entropy and peak fraction also show some variation, making it difficult to determine if this is a seizure.

跨通道 / C3.cross_channel：The cross-channel evidence shows a decrease in correlation between the selected channels during the interval [740.0, 770.0]. The correlation between P8-O2 and P7-O1 decreased from 0.077 to 0.0458, while the correlation between P8-O2 and P4-O2 increased from 0.5677 to 0.6861. This suggests that the activity is not globally synchronized, which is consistent with a focal seizure. However, the correlation between P4-O2 and C4-P4 decreased from 0.2912 to 0.2572, which could indicate a change in the activity pattern.

替代解释：The changes in the signal could be due to a physiological state change or an artifact. The spectral changes could also be due to a shift in brain activity, but the lack of consistent changes across all channels makes it difficult to determine if this is a seizure.

C4 | uncertain | 795.0–830.0秒

时域 / C4.temporal：The temporal evidence shows a significant increase in RMS and line-length ratios for most channels during the interval, with a notable peak at 810.0-815.0 seconds. This suggests a possible change in activity, but the changes are not sustained across the entire interval and are not consistently elevated.

频谱 / C4.spectral：The spectral analysis indicates a shift in delta power and a decrease in entropy during the interval, which could suggest a change in brain activity. However, the changes are not consistent across all channels and do not show a clear pattern of rhythmicity or sustained elevation.

跨通道 / C4.cross_channel：The cross-channel correlations show some changes, but they are not consistent across all pairs. The correlations are not strong enough to suggest a focal seizure, and the changes are not sustained across the entire interval.

替代解释：The changes observed could be due to physiological variations or artifacts. The lack of sustained rhythmicity and the presence of high entropy in some channels suggest that the changes may not be seizure-related.

时域 / C1.temporal：The temporal evidence shows a sustained increase in RMS and line-length ratios across the channels F4-C4, FT10-T8, and F8-T8 during the interval [110.0, 145.0]. However, the ratios return to baseline levels after 145.0 seconds, indicating that the change is not sustained throughout the entire interval. The RMS ratio for F4-C4 increases from 1.4679 to 1.567 during the interval, but drops back to 1.3532 after 145.0 seconds. This suggests that the change is not consistent and may be due to transient activity rather than a sustained event.

频谱 / C1.spectral：The spectral evidence shows a decrease in delta relative power and an increase in theta relative power during the interval [110.0, 145.0]. However, these changes are not consistent across all channels. For example, the delta relative power for F4-C4 decreases from 0.6147 to 0.4993, while the theta relative power increases from 0.249 to 0.3647. These changes are not sustained throughout the interval and may be due to transient activity rather than a sustained event. Additionally, the spectral entropy and peak fraction do not show significant changes that would indicate a seizure.

跨通道 / C1.cross_channel：The cross-channel evidence shows a decrease in pairwise correlations during the interval [110.0, 145.0]. For example, the correlation between F4-C4 and FP2-F8 decreases from 0.4954 to 0.3982, while the correlation between F4-C4 and FT10-T8 decreases from 0.3736 to -0.0246. These changes are not consistent across all channels and may be due to transient activity rather than a sustained event. Additionally, the shared electrodes can create correlation, which may inflate the correlation values and make it difficult to interpret the evidence.

替代解释：The changes observed in the temporal, spectral, and cross-channel evidence could be due to transient activity rather than a sustained event. The changes are not consistent across all channels and may be due to physiological state changes or artifacts. The spectral entropy and peak fraction do not show significant changes that would indicate a seizure. Therefore, the evidence is not sufficient to support a seizure diagnosis.

C3 | uncertain | 430.0–455.0秒

时域 / C3.temporal：The temporal evidence shows a sustained increase in RMS and line-length ratios across the selected channels (F8-T8, FT9-FT10, FT10-T8, F4-C4) during the interval [430.0, 455.0]. The ratios increased from 1.44 to 1.465 for F8-T8, 1.4183 to 1.465 for FT9-FT10, and 1.4117 to 1.465 for F4-C4, indicating a consistent change in activity. However, the change is not sufficiently sustained or rhythmic to meet the minimum support criteria.

频谱 / C3.spectral：The spectral evidence shows a shift in the dominant frequency and changes in the relative power distribution during the interval. The dominant frequency increased from 2.0 Hz to 3.0 Hz, and the delta relative power decreased from 0.7017 to 0.5744. However, the changes are not consistent across all channels, and the spectral entropy increased, which may indicate a more complex pattern rather than a seizure.

跨通道 / C3.cross_channel：The cross-channel evidence shows a decrease in pairwise correlations during the interval, particularly between F8-T8 and FT9-FT10, which may indicate a change in the synchronization of activity. However, the correlations are not consistently low across all pairs, and the shared electrodes may have influenced the results. The changes are not sufficient to confirm a seizure.

替代解释：The changes observed could be due to a physiological state change or an artifact. The spectral changes may reflect a shift in brain activity rather than a seizure. The cross-channel correlations may be influenced by shared electrodes, and the temporal changes may not be sustained or rhythmic enough to confirm a seizure.

C4 | suspected | 1130.0–1200.0秒

时域 / C4.temporal：The temporal evidence shows a significant increase in RMS and line-length ratios across multiple channels (FT10-T8: 2.7171 → 2938.7391, F8-T8: 2.5418 → 3909.5894, T8-P8-0: 2.1934 → 3259.3941, T7-P7: 2.1999 → 2632.3051) from 1130.0 to 1200.0 seconds, indicating a sustained change in activity.

频谱 / C4.spectral：The spectral evidence shows a marked decrease in delta relative power (FT10-T8: 0.7776 → 0.5008, F8-T8: 0.7603 → 0.5233, T8-P8-0: 0.676 → 0.5413, T7-P7: 0.6764 → 0.6317) and an increase in beta and gamma relative power (FT10-T8: 0.0152 → 0.158, F8-T8: 0.0113 → 0.1401, T8-P8-0: 0.0145 → 0.1145, T7-P7: 0.0138 → 0.0388) during the interval, suggesting a shift in activity patterns.

跨通道 / C4.cross_channel：The cross-channel evidence shows a decrease in correlation between channels (FT10-T8 and F8-T8: 0.8166 → 0.6907, FT10-T8 and T8-P8-0: -0.1076 → -0.3221, F8-T8 and T8-P8-0: -0.0804 → -0.0657, T8-P8-0 and T7-P7: 0.229 → 0.1936) during the interval, indicating a possible focal change in activity.

替代解释：The observed changes could be due to a focal seizure, but other explanations such as physiological changes or artifacts should be considered. The sustained changes in RMS and line-length ratios, along with the shift in spectral power, support the possibility of a seizure.
